# NYC What To Do

**Rolling window:** 2026-09-24 through 2026-09-30  
**Updated:** 2026-09-24T10:46:06+00:00  
**Book-ahead watch:** through 2027-01-22

## Best date plans

### 2026-09-29 — Rooftop Yoga at Brooklyn Grange Navy Yard + Grimm Tavern

**Rooftop Yoga at Brooklyn Grange Navy Yard, then dinner.** American tavern and brewery at Grimm Tavern. It is 2.3 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 6 pm, so eat afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=Brooklyn+Navy+Yard%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY&destination=990+Washington+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11225&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://luma.com/brooklyngrange.navyyards) · [Restaurant](https://grimmales.com/location/grimm-tavern/)

### 2026-09-24 — Public Craft Night at NYC Resistor + Lilia

**Public Craft Night at NYC Resistor, then dinner.** Italian at Lilia. It is 3.0 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It runs to 9:30 pm, so this is a late stop afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=87+3rd+Ave%2C+4th+Floor%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11217&destination=567+Union+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11211&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.eventbrite.com/e/public-craft-night-mondays-and-thursdays-tickets-162729866411) · [Restaurant](https://www.lilianewyork.com/)

### 2026-09-25 — Grimm Tavern + Sean Hayes with Etienne de Rocher

**Dinner, then Sean Hayes with Etienne de Rocher.** American tavern and brewery at Grimm Tavern. It is 1.4 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. Sean Hayes with Etienne de Rocher starts at 7 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=990+Washington+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11225&destination=635+Sackett+St%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11217&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://lpr.com/lpr_events/sh26bk/) · [Event booking](https://kyd.to/p6XMMttG) · [Restaurant](https://grimmales.com/location/grimm-tavern/)

### 2026-09-27 — Smorgasburg Prospect Park + WINNER in the Park

**Smorgasburg Prospect Park, then a meal.** Bakery and cafe at WINNER in the Park. It is a 12-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 11 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=Breeze+Hill%2C+Prospect+Park+%28enter+at+Lincoln+Road%29%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY&destination=The+Picnic+House%2C+Prospect+Park%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11215&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.smorgasburg.com/) · [Restaurant](https://www.winner.nyc/)

### 2026-09-30 — Lilia + Salon Parcha Featuring Ella Bric & Special Guests

**Dinner, then Salon Parcha Featuring Ella Bric & Special Guests.** Italian at Lilia. It is a 10-minute walk from the venue. Salon Parcha Featuring Ella Bric & Special Guests starts at 8 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=567+Union+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11211&destination=80+N.+6th+Street&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.nationalsawdust.org/event/salon-parcha-featuring-ella-bric-special-guests) · [Event booking](https://ci.ovationtix.com/36490/production/1291141?performanceId=11868138) · [Restaurant](https://www.lilianewyork.com/)

### 2026-09-28 — Gage & Tollner + A Winged Victory for the Sullen with Pan•American

**Dinner, then A Winged Victory for the Sullen with Pan•American.** American chophouse and seafood at Gage & Tollner. It is 1.6 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. A Winged Victory for the Sullen with Pan•American starts at 8 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=372-374+Fulton+St%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11201&destination=159+Pioneer+St%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11231&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://pioneerworks.org/events) · [Event booking](https://www.songkick.com/concerts/43230698-a-winged-victory-for-the-sullen-at-pioneer-works) · [Restaurant](https://www.gageandtollner.com/)

### 2026-09-27 — Miss Ada + Francesca Anderegg and Erika Ribeiro: Images of Brazil

**Dinner, then Francesca Anderegg and Erika Ribeiro: Images of Brazil.** Mediterranean at Miss Ada. It is 2.1 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. Francesca Anderegg and Erika Ribeiro: Images of Brazil starts at 7:30 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=184+Dekalb+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11205&destination=80+N.+6th+Street&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.nationalsawdust.org/event/francesca-anderegg-and-erika-ribeiro-images-of-brazil) · [Event booking](https://ci.ovationtix.com/36490/production/1280441?performanceId=11829118) · [Restaurant](https://www.missadanyc.com/)

### 2026-09-24 — Black Forest Brooklyn + Chronovisor

**Dinner, then Chronovisor.** German at Black Forest Brooklyn. It is a 3-minute walk from the venue. Chronovisor starts at 7 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=733+Fulton+St%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11217&destination=30+Lafayette+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11217&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.bam.org/film/2026/chronovisor) · [Event booking](https://commerce.bam.org/) · [Restaurant](https://www.blackforestbrooklyn.com/) · [Reservations](https://www.blackforestbrooklyn.com/oktoberfest)

#### [Bryant Park Birding Tour](https://bryantpark.org/activities/birding-tours)

5:00 PM · outdoors · Free · Bryant Park Info Kiosk · Midtown · Manhattan · Score 46.0  
A free Friday migration-season walk with urban naturalist Gabriel Willow, looking for birds passing through one of Midtown's small but productive green spaces.
**Timing:** Tours are Tuesdays at 8am and Thursdays and Fridays at 5pm during migration season.
**Practical note:** NWS forecast gives a chance of showers after 2pm and breezy north winds; bring a rain layer and confirm conditions before leaving.
**Why now:** Bryant Park schedules Friday evening birding tours during migration season.
